null;\n  postalCode?: string | null;\n}\n\nexport interface Amenity {\n  id: string;\n  label: string;\n  icon?: string | null; // Lucide icon name\n  category?: 'Food and Drink' | 'Wellness' | 'Business' | 'Transport' | 'Rooms' | 'Family' | 'Accessibility' | 'Leisure' | 'Other';\n  available?: boolean;\n  detail?: string | null;\n  venueName?: string | null;\n}\n\nexport interface Image {\n  id: string;\n  url: string;\n  alt: string;\n  caption?: string | null;\n  credit?: string | null;\n  isHero?: boolean;\n}\n\nexport interface DimensionScore {\n  label: string;\n  score: number;\n  maxScore: number;\n  weight: number; // percentage (e.g., 15)\n}\n\nexport interface Scorecard {\n  totalScore: number;\n  dimensions: DimensionScore[];\n  confidence: 'DMW Researched' | 'DMW Visited' | 'DMW Revisited';\n}\n\nexport interface HotelSummary {\n  id: string;\n  slug: string;\n  edition: number;\n  rank: number;\n  rankStatus?: 'illustrative' | 'final';\n  name: string;\n  location: Location;\n  archetype: Archetype;\n  publicationStatus: 'draft' | 'review' | 'published' | 'archived' | 'prototype-ready' | 'research-draft';\n  featured: boolean;\n  strategicLens?: string;\n  dmwJudgement?: string | null;\n  assessmentPendingLabel?: string | null;\n  indicativeRate?: {\n    currency: string;\n    amount: number;\n    label: string;\n    basis: string;\n  } | null;\n  businessTravelSuitability?: string;\n  scores?: Scorecard | null;\n  essentialAmenities: Amenity[];\n  distinctions: Distinction[];\n  hasFieldReport?: boolean;\n  hasStrategicFeature: boolean;\n  hasPricingAnalysis: boolean;\n  primaryImage?: Image | null;\n  profileUrl: string;\n  articleUrl?: string | null;\n}\n\nexport interface FieldReport {\n  id: string;\n  visitDate: string; // YYYY-MM\n  nights: number;\n  approximateRatePaid?: number | null;\n  currency?: string | null;\n  roomCategory?: string | null;\n  travelPurpose?: string | null;\n  arrivalObservation: string;\n  roomObservation: string;\n  serviceObservation: string;\n  atmosphereObservation: string;\n  amenitiesUsed: string[];\n  whatWorked: string;\n  whatDisappointed: string;\n  returnIntention: string;\n  thesisConfirmation: string;\n}\n\nexport interface PricePoint {\n  date: string; // YYYY-MM-DD\n  rate: number;\n  available?: boolean | null;\n  roomCategory?: string | null;\n  rateType?: string | null;\n  notes?: string | null;\n}\n\nexport interface EventMarker {\n  date: string;\n  label: string;\n  eventType?: string | null;\n}\n\nexport interface PricingIntelligence {\n  status: 'not-started' | 'collecting' | 'complete';\n  currency?: string | null;\n  roomBasis?: string | null;\n  occupancyBasis?: string | null;\n  taxesIncluded?: boolean | null;\n  observationStart?: string | null;\n  observationEnd?: string | null;\n  collectionDate?: string | null;\n  sourceMethod?: string | null;\n  medianObservedRate?: number | null;\n  lowestObservedRate?: number | null;\n  highestObservedRate?: number | null;\n  bestValuePeriod?: string | null;\n  peakPeriod?: string | null;\n  weekdayWeekendObservation?: string | null;\n  dmwInterpretation?: string | null;\n  dataPoints: PricePoint[];\n  eventMarkers: EventMarker[];\n  limitations?: string | null;\n}\n\n// Full profile, combining summary fields + schema requirements for the profile page\nexport interface HotelProfile extends Omit<HotelSummary, 'essentialAmenities'> {\n  dmwOverview?: string | null;\n  inclusionRationale?: string | null;\n  centralStrength?: string | null;\n  centralQuestion?: string | null;\n  bestSuitedFor: string[];\n  \n  identity: {\n    brand?: string | null;\n    collection?: string | null;\n    operator?: string | null;\n    owner?: string | null;\n    ownershipPubliclyConfirmed: boolean;\n    designer?: string | null;\n    architect?: string | null;\n  };\n  \n  propertyFacts: {\n    openingYear?: number | null;\n    lastMajorRenovationYear?: number | null;\n    roomCount?: number | null;\n    suiteCount?: number | null;\n    checkInTime?: string | null;\n    checkOutTime?: string | null;\n    propertyType?: string | null;\n    seasonality?: string | null;\n  };\n  \n  rates: {\n    currency?: string | null;\n    indicativeFrom?: number | null;\n    indicativeTo?: number | null;\n    typicalRate?: number | null;\n    rateLabel?: string | null;\n    taxesIncluded?: boolean | null;\n    rateBasis?: string | null;\n    rateObservedAt?: string | null;\n  };\n\n  amenities: Amenity[];\n  \n  venues: Array<{\n    name: string;\n    type: string;\n    description?: string | null;\n    strategicRole?: string | null;\n    mealPeriods: string[];\n    attractsNonResidents?: boolean | null;\n    recognition?: string | null;\n    officialUrl?: string | null;\n  }>;\n  \n  analysis: {\n    hospitalityProposition?: string | null;\n    atmosphere?: string | null;\n    intendedClientele?: string | null;\n    designLogic?: string | null;\n    locationLogic?: string | null;\n    hospitalityStrategy?: string | null;\n    revenueStrategy?: string | null;\n    pricingPowerThesis?: string | null;\n    assetLogic?: string | null;\n    competitiveMoat?: string | null;\n    strategicOpportunity?: string | null;\n    investorQuestion?: string | null;\n  };\n  \n  businessTravel: {\n    summary?: string | null;\n    suitability?: 'Exceptional' | 'Strong' | 'Good' | 'Limited' | 'Not Assessed' | null;\n    locationEfficiency?: string | null;\n    workspace?: string | null;\n    connectivity?: string | null;\n    meetingSuitability?: string | null;\n    privacy?: string | null;\n    sleepAndNoise?: string | null;\n    serviceSpeed?: string | null;\n    breakfastPracticality?: string | null;\n    soloTravellerSuitability?: string | null;\n  };\n  \n  rooms: {\n    summary?: string | null;\n    minimumSizeSqm?: number | null;\n    maximumStandardRoomSizeSqm?: number | null;\n    categories: string[];\n    workspace?: string | null;\n    storage?: string | null;\n    bathroom?: string | null;\n    lighting?: string | null;\n    technology?: string | null;\n    views?: string | null;\n    spatialStrength?: string | null;\n    spatialCompromise?: string | null;\n  };\n\n  pricingIntelligence?: PricingIntelligence | null;\n  \n  competitiveSet: Array<{\n    hotelId?: string | null;\n    name: string;\n    location?: string | null;\n    relativePricePosition?: string | null;\n    locationDifference?: string | null;\n    amenityDifference?: string | null;\n    brandDifference?: string | null;\n    dmwInterpretation?: string | null;\n  }>;\n\n  fieldReports: FieldReport[];\n  images: Image[];\n  \n  links: {\n    officialWebsite?: string | null;\n    bookingUrl?: string | null;\n    pressPage?: string | null;\n  };\n  \n  sources: Array<{\n    type: 'official' | 'third-party' | 'financial' | 'pricing' | 'other';\n    name: string;\n    url?: string | null;\n    dateAccessed?: string | null;\n    notes?: string | null;\n  }>;\n}\n\nexport interface IndexData {\n  edition: number;\n  editionStatus: string;\n  title: string;\n  publisher: string;\n  prototypeNotice: string;\n  hotels: HotelSummary[];\n}\n\nexport interface Collection {\n  slug: string;\n  title: string;\n  edition: string;\n  description: string;\n  hotels: {"content":"import React, { useState, useMemo, useEffect } from 'react';\nimport { useParams, Navigate } from 'react-router-dom';\nimport { Container } from '../components/shared';\nimport { RankedHotelItem } from '../components/index/RankedHotelItem';\nimport { getCollection } from '../data/api';\nimport styles from './CollectionIndex.module.css';\n\nexport const CollectionIndex: React.FC = () => {\n  const { slug } = useParams<{ slug: string }>();\n  const collection = getCollection(slug || '');\n  \n  // Reset filters when changing collections\n  const [search, setSearch] = useState('');\n  const [regionFilter, setRegionFilter] = useState('All');\n  const [priceFilter, setPriceFilter] = useState('All Prices');\n  const [lensFilter, setLensFilter] = useState('All Lenses');\n  const [amenityFilters, setAmenityFilters] = useState<string[]>([]);\n  const [savedPresetActive, setSavedPresetActive] = useState(false);\n\n  useEffect(() => {\n    try {\n      const saved = localStorage.getItem('dmw_saved_filters');\n      if (saved) {\n        const parsed = JSON.parse(saved);\n        if (parsed.slug === slug) {\n          setSearch(parsed.search || '');\n          setRegionFilter(parsed.regionFilter || 'All');\n          setPriceFilter(parsed.priceFilter || 'All Prices');\n          setLensFilter(parsed.lensFilter || 'All Lenses');\n          setAmenityFilters(parsed.amenityFilters || []);\n          setSavedPresetActive(true);\n          return;\n        }\n      }\n    } catch (e) {}\n    \n    // Reset if no preset\n    setSearch('');\n    setRegionFilter('All');\n    setPriceFilter('All Prices');\n    setLensFilter('All Lenses');\n    setAmenityFilters([]);\n    setSavedPresetActive(false);\n  }, [slug]);\n\n  const savePreset = () => {\n    localStorage.setItem('dmw_saved_filters', JSON.stringify({\n      slug, search, regionFilter, priceFilter, lensFilter, amenityFilters\n    }));\n    setSavedPresetActive(true);\n  };\n\n  const clearPreset = () => {\n    localStorage.removeItem('dmw_saved_filters');\n    setSearch('');\n    setRegionFilter('All');\n    setPriceFilter('All Prices');\n    setLensFilter('All Lenses');\n    setAmenityFilters([]);\n    setSavedPresetActive(false);\n  };\n\n  if (!collection) {\n    return <Navigate to=\"/collections/the-global-100\" replace />;\n  }\n\n  const allHotels = collection.hotels;\n  \n  const regions = ['All', ...Array.from(new Set(allHotels.map(h => h.location.region)))];\n  \n  const priceBands = [\n    { label: 'All Prices', max: Infinity },\n    { label: 'Under $500', max: 500 },\n    { label: 'Under $1,000', max: 1000 },\n    { label: 'Under $1,500', max: 1500 },\n    { label: '$1,500+', min: 1500, max: Infinity }\n  ];\n\n  const strategicLenses = ['All Lenses', ...Array.from(new Set(allHotels.map(h => h.strategicLens).filter(Boolean)))];\n  \n  const amenityOptions = [\n    { id: 'gym', label: 'Gym' },\n    { id: 'parking', label: 'Parking' },\n    { id: 'restaurant', label: 'Restaurant' }\n  ];\n\n  const toggleAmenity = (id: string) => {\n    setAmenityFilters(prev => \n      prev.includes(id) ? prev.filter(a => a !== id) : [...prev, id]\n    );\n  };\n\n  const filteredHotels = useMemo(() => {\n    return allHotels.filter(hotel => {\n      const matchesSearch = hotel.name.toLowerCase().includes(search.toLowerCase()) || \n                            hotel.location.city.toLowerCase().includes(search.toLowerCase());\n      const matchesRegion = regionFilter === 'All' || hotel.location.region === regionFilter;\n      \n      let matchesPrice = true;\n      if (priceFilter !== 'All Prices') {\n        const rate = hotel.indicativeRate?.amount || 0;\n        const band = priceBands.find(b => b.label === priceFilter);\n        if (band) {\n          matchesPrice = rate > 0 && rate <= band.max && (band.min ? rate >= band.min : true);\n        }\n      }\n\n      const matchesLens = lensFilter === 'All Lenses' || hotel.strategicLens === lensFilter;\n      \n      const matchesAmenities = amenityFilters.every(amenityId => {\n        const amenity = hotel.essentialAmenities?.find(a => a.id === amenityId);\n        return amenity && amenity.available === true;\n      });\n\n      return matchesSearch && matchesRegion && matchesPrice && matchesLens && matchesAmenities;\n    });\n  }, [allHotels, search, regionFilter, priceFilter]);\n\n  return (\n    <div className={styles.page}>\n      <Container variant=\"standard\">\n        <div className={styles.masthead}>\n          <h1 className={styles.title}>{collection.title}</h1>\n          <p className={styles.subtitle}>{collection.description}</p>\n        </div>\n\n        <div className={styles.controls}>\n          <input \n            type=\"text\" \n            placeholder=\"Search hotels or cities...\" \n            value={search}\n            onChange={e => setSearch(e.target.value)}\n            className={styles.searchInput}\n          />\n          \n          <div className={styles.filtersContainer}>\n            <div className={styles.filters}>\n              {regions.map(region => (\n                <button \n                  key={region}\n                  className={`${styles.filterBtn} ${regionFilter === region ? styles.active : ''}`}\n                  onClick={() => setRegionFilter(region)}\n                >\n                  {region}\n                </button>\n              ))}\n            </div>\n            \n            <div className={styles.filters}>\n              {priceBands.map(band => (\n                <button \n                  key={band.label}\n                  className={`${styles.filterBtn} ${priceFilter === band.label ? styles.active : ''}`}\n                  onClick={() => setPriceFilter(band.label)}\n                >\n                  {band.label}\n                </button>\n              ))}\n            </div>\n            \n            <div className={styles.filters}>\n              {strategicLenses.map(lens => (\n                <button \n                  key={lens as string}\n                  className={`${styles.filterBtn} ${lensFilter === lens ? styles.active : ''}`}\n                  onClick={() => setLensFilter(lens as string)}\n                >\n                  {lens}\n                </button>\n              ))}\n            </div>\n\n            <div className={styles.filters}>\n              <span className={styles.filterLabel} style={{marginRight: '0.5rem', fontSize: '0.875rem', color: 'var(--text-secondary)'}}>Amenities:</span>\n              {amenityOptions.map(opt => (\n                <button \n                  key={opt.id}\n                  className={`${styles.filterBtn} ${amenityFilters.includes(opt.id) ? styles.active : ''}`}\n                  onClick={() => toggleAmenity(opt.id)}\n                >\n                  {opt.label}\n                </button>\n              ))}\n            </div>\n            \n            <div className={styles.filters} style={{ marginLeft: 'auto' }}>\n              {!savedPresetActive ? (\n                <button onClick={savePreset} style={{ fontSize: '0.875rem', textDecoration: 'underline', color: 'var(--text-secondary)', background: 'none', border: 'none', cursor: 'pointer' }}>\n                  Save Preset\n                </button>\n              ) : (\n                <button onClick={clearPreset} style={{ fontSize: '0.875rem', textDecoration: 'underline', color: 'var(--accent)', background: 'none', border: 'none', cursor: 'pointer' }}>\n                  Clear Preset\n                </button>\n              )}\n            </div>\n          </div>\n        </div>\n\n        <div className={styles.resultsCount}>\n          Showing {filteredHotels.length} {filteredHotels.length === 1 ? 'hotel' : 'hotels'}\n          {regionFilter !== 'All' && ` in ${regionFilter}`}\n          {priceFilter !== 'All Prices' && ` (${priceFilter.toLowerCase()})`}\n          {search && ` matching \"${search}\"`}\n        </div>\n\n        <div className={styles.list}>\n          {filteredHotels.length > 0 ? (\n            filteredHotels.map(hotel => (\n              <RankedHotelItem key={hotel.id} hotel={hotel} />\n            ))\n          ) : (\n            <div className={styles.empty}>\n              <p>No hotels match your filters.</p>\n              <button onClick={() => {setSearch(''); setRegionFilter('All');}} className={styles.resetBtn}>\n                Clear filters\n              </button>\n            </div>\n          )}\n        </div>\n      </Container>\n    </div>\n  );\n};\n","path":"/Users/stevenmorales/Development/hotel100/src/pages/CollectionIndex.tsx","size_bytes":8291,"truncated":false}

{"content":"import React from 'react';\nimport { useParams, Link } from 'react-router-dom';\nimport { ArrowLeft, MapPin, Quote } from 'lucide-react';\nimport { Container, Badge, IconLabel, SectionHeader, Button } from '../components/shared';\nimport { PriceCurveChart } from '../components/hotel/PriceCurveChart';\nimport { Scorecard } from '../components/hotel/Scorecard';\nimport { getHotelProfile } from '../data/api';\nimport styles from './HotelProfile.module.css';\n\nexport const HotelProfile: React.FC = () => {\n  const { slug } = useParams<{ slug: string }>();\n  const hotel = getHotelProfile(slug || '');\n\n  if (!hotel) {\n    return (\n      <Container variant=\"standard\" className={styles.notFound}>\n        <div className={styles.notFoundContent}>\n          <h2>Profile in Preparation</h2>\n          <p>The comprehensive DMW assessment for this property is currently being compiled.</p>\n          <Link to=\"/the-100\" className={styles.backLink}>\n            <ArrowLeft size={16} /> Back to Index\n          </Link>\n        </div>\n      </Container>\n    );\n  }\n\n  return (\n    <article className={styles.profile}>\n      {/* Hero Section */}\n      <header className={styles.hero}>\n        <Container variant=\"standard\">\n          <Link to=\"/the-100\" className={styles.backLink}>\n            <ArrowLeft size={16} /> Back to Index\n          </Link>\n          \n          <div className={styles.heroContent}>\n            <div className={styles.heroMeta}>\n              <Badge label={`Rank ${hotel.rank}`} type=\"edition\" />\n            </div>\n            <h1 className={styles.title}>{hotel.name}</h1>\n            <p className={styles.location}>\n              <MapPin size={18} /> {hotel.location.displayLocation}\n            </p>\n            {hotel.inclusionRationale && (\n              <div className={styles.heroRationale}>\n                <Quote size={20} className={styles.rationaleIcon} strokeWidth={1.5} />\n                <p>{hotel.inclusionRationale}</p>\n              </div>\n            )}\n          </div>\n        </Container>\n      </header>\n\n      <Container variant=\"reading\" className={styles.mainContent}>\n        {/* Fact Grid (Property Intelligence) moved before overview */}\n        <section className={styles.section}>\n          <SectionHeader title=\"Property Intelligence\" />\n          <div className={styles.factGrid}>\n            <IconLabel iconName=\"Target\" label=\"Strategic Lens\" value={hotel.strategicLens} />\n            <IconLabel iconName=\"Building\" label=\"Archetype\" value={hotel.archetype} />\n            <IconLabel iconName=\"Calendar\" label=\"Opened\" value={hotel.propertyFacts.openingYear?.toString()} />\n            <IconLabel iconName=\"BedDouble\" label=\"Rooms\" value={hotel.propertyFacts.roomCount?.toString()} />\n            <IconLabel iconName=\"Users\" label=\"Operator\" value={hotel.identity.brand || hotel.identity.operator} />\n            <IconLabel iconName=\"Key\" label=\"Ownership\" value={hotel.identity.owner} />\n            <IconLabel iconName=\"MapPin\" label=\"Neighbourhood\" value={hotel.location.neighbourhood} />\n          </div>\n        </section>\n\n        {/* Core Thesis / Overview moved after Property Intelligence */}\n        <section className={styles.section}>\n          <p className={styles.overviewText}>{hotel.dmwOverview}</p>\n        </section>\n\n        {/* Editorial Sections */}\n\n        {hotel.analysis && (\n          <section className={styles.section}>\n            <SectionHeader title=\"Strategic Analysis\" />\n            <div className={styles.prose}>\n              {hotel.analysis.hospitalityProposition && (\n                <>\n                  <h3>Hospitality Proposition</h3>\n                  <p>{hotel.analysis.hospitalityProposition}</p>\n                </>\n              )}\n              {hotel.analysis.revenueStrategy && (\n                <>\n                  <h3>Revenue Strategy</h3>\n                  <p>{hotel.analysis.revenueStrategy}</p>\n                </>\n              )}\n            </div>\n          </section>\n        )}\n\n        {/* Scorecard */}\n        {hotel.scores && (\n          <section className={styles.section}>\n            <Scorecard scores={hotel.scores} />\n          </section>\n        )}\n\n        {/* Pricing Intelligence */}\n        {hotel.pricingIntelligence && (\n          <section className={styles.section}>\n            <SectionHeader \n              title=\"Pricing Intelligence\" \n              subtitle=\"Observation of publicly available forward rates to identify pricing power and revenue strategy.\"\n            />\n            <PriceCurveChart pricing={hotel.pricingIntelligence} />\n          </section>\n        )}\n\n        {/* Optional firsthand note; never part of ranking or profile completeness. */}\n        {hotel.fieldReports && hotel.fieldReports.length > 0 && (\n          <section className={styles.section}>\n            <SectionHeader title=\"A Note From Our Stay\" />\n            {hotel.fieldReports.map(report => (\n              <div key={report.id} className={styles.fieldReport}>\n                <div className={styles.reportHeader}>\n                  <Badge label=\"Field Report\" type=\"field-report\" />\n                  <span className={styles.reportDate}>{report.visitDate}</span>\n                </div>\n                <div className={styles.reportGrid}>\n                  <div>\n                    <span className={styles.reportLabel}>Arrival</span>\n                    <p className={styles.reportText}>{report.arrivalObservation}</p>\n                  </div>\n                  <div>\n                    <span className={styles.reportLabel}>Room</span>\n                    <p className={styles.reportText}>{report.roomObservation}</p>\n                  </div>\n                  <div>\n                    <span className={styles.reportLabel}>Service</span>\n                    <p className={styles.reportText}>{report.serviceObservation}</p>\n                  </div>\n                  <div>\n                    <span className={styles.reportLabel}>Atmosphere</span>\n                    <p className={styles.reportText}>{report.atmosphereObservation}</p>\n                  </div>\n                </div>\n                \n                <div className={styles.reportVerdict}>\n                  <div>\n                    <span className={styles.reportLabel}>What Worked</span>\n                    <p className={styles.reportText}>{report.whatWorked}</p>\n                  </div>\n                  <div>\n                    <span className={styles.reportLabel}>What Disappointed</span>\n                    <p className={styles.reportText}>{report.whatDisappointed}</p>\n                  </div>\n                </div>\n\n                <div className={styles.thesisSection}>\n                  <span className={styles.reportLabel}>Thesis Confirmation</span>\n                  <p className={styles.thesisText}>{report.thesisConfirmation}</p>\n                </div>\n              </div>\n            ))}\n          </section>\n        )}\n\n        {/* Action */}\n        {hotel.links?.officialWebsite && (\n          <section className={styles.section}>\n            <a \n              href={hotel.links.officialWebsite} \n              target=\"_blank\" \n              rel=\"noopener noreferrer\"\n              className={styles.buttonLink}\n            >\n              <Button external>\n                Visit Official Website\n              </Button>\n            </a>\n          </section>\n        )}\n      </Container>\n    </article>\n  );\n};\n","path":"/Users/stevenmorales/Development/hotel100/src/pages/HotelProfile.tsx","size_bytes":7417,"truncated":false}

{"content":"import React from 'react';\nimport { Link } from 'react-router-dom';\nimport type { HotelSummary, DimensionScore } from '../../data/types';\nimport { Badge } from '../shared/Badge';\nimport { MapPin, Building, ArrowRight, Tag, Target } from 'lucide-react';\nimport styles from './RankedHotelItem.module.css';\n\ninterface RankedHotelItemProps {\n  hotel: HotelSummary;\n}\n\nexport const RankedHotelItem: React.FC<RankedHotelItemProps> = ({ hotel }) => {\n  // Get top 3 dimensions to highlight\n  let topDimensions: DimensionScore[] = [];\n  if (hotel.scores?.dimensions) {\n    // Sort by raw score or percentage of max score? Percentage makes more sense for highlighting strengths.\n    topDimensions = [...hotel.scores.dimensions]\n      .sort((a, b) => (b.score / b.maxScore) - (a.score / a.maxScore))\n      .slice(0, 3);\n  }\n\n  return (\n    <article className={styles.item}>\n      <div className={styles.rankColumn}>\n        <span className={styles.rankNumber}>{hotel.rank}</span>\n      </div>\n      \n      <div className={styles.mainColumn}>\n        <div className={styles.headerRow}>\n          <h3 className={styles.name}>\n            <Link to={hotel.profileUrl} className={styles.link}>\n              {hotel.name}\n            </Link>\n          </h3>\n          {hotel.featured && <Badge label=\"Featured Analysis\" type=\"category\" />}\n        </div>\n\n        <div className={styles.metaRow}>\n          <span className={styles.metaItem}>\n            <MapPin size={14} />\n            {hotel.location.displayLocation}\n          </span>\n          <span className={styles.metaItem}>\n            <Building size={14} />\n            {hotel.archetype}\n          </span>\n          {hotel.indicativeRate && (\n            <span className={styles.metaItem}>\n              <Tag size={14} />\n              ~${hotel.indicativeRate.amount} / nt\n            </span>\n          )}\n          {hotel.strategicLens && (\n            <span className={styles.metaItem}>\n              <Target size={14} />\n              {hotel.strategicLens}\n            </span>\n          )}\n        </div>\n\n        {hotel.dmwJudgement && (\n          <p className={styles.judgement}>{hotel.dmwJudgement}</p>\n        )}\n\n        <div className={styles.distinctions}>\n          {hotel.distinctions.map((dist) => (\n            <Badge key={dist} label={dist} type=\"distinction\" />\n          ))}\n        </div>\n      </div>\n\n      {/* New Dimension Highlights Column */}\n      <div className={styles.highlightsColumn}>\n        {topDimensions.length > 0 && (\n          <div className={styles.highlights}>\n            <span className={styles.highlightsLabel}>Key Strengths</span>\n            {topDimensions.map((dim, i) => (\n              <div key={i} className={styles.highlightItem}>\n                <span className={styles.highlightName}>{dim.label}</span>\n                <div className={styles.highlightBar}>\n                  <div \n                    className={styles.highlightFill} \n                    style={{ width: `${(dim.score / dim.maxScore) * 100}%` }}\n                  />\n                </div>\n              </div>\n            ))}\n          </div>\n        )}\n      </div>\n\n      {/* New Score Column */}\n      <div className={styles.scoreColumn}>\n        {hotel.scores && (\n          <div className={styles.scoreDisplay}>\n            <span {"content":"import type { IndexData, HotelProfile, HotelSummary, Collection } from './types';\nimport indexDataRaw from '../../07-content/hotels.json';\nimport collectionsDataRaw from '../../07-content/collections.json';\n\nconst indexData = indexDataRaw as unknown as IndexData;\nconst collectionsData = collectionsDataRaw as unknown as { collections: Collection[] };\n\n/**\n * Returns the index data containing the ranking of 100 hotels (prototype).\n */\nexport function getIndexData(): Omit<IndexData, 'hotels'> {\n  const { hotels, ...meta } = indexData;\n  return meta;\n}\n\n/**\n * Returns all hotels from the index.\n */\nexport function getAllHotels(): HotelSummary[] {\n  return indexData.hotels;\n}\n\n/**\n * Returns a collection by its slug.\n */\nexport function getCollection(slug: string): Collection | undefined {\n  return collectionsData.collections.find(c => c.slug === slug);\n}\n\n/**\n * Returns a specific hotel profile by its slug.\n */\nexport function getHotelProfile(slug: string): HotelProfile | undefined {\n  // Try to find the hotel in the master index\n  const hotel = indexData.hotels.find(h => h.slug === slug);\n  \n  if (hotel) {\n    return hotel as unknown as HotelProfile;\n  }\n  return undefined;\n}\n","path":"/Users/stevenmorales/Development/hotel100/src/data/api.ts","size_bytes":1192,"truncated":false}

The review surfaced two concrete regressions from the recent commits: the new collection filters were not fully wired into the memoized result set, and the new “Check Dates” CTA was a dead `href="#"` link with no backing data source.

<!--prism:tool-calls
[{"id":"call_RjkS5S4gy91pP1WkHOI6O1Fv","type":"function","function":{"name":"grep","arguments":"{\"case_insensitive\":false,\"context_lines\":2,\"glob\":\"07-content/*.json\",\"max_results\":50,\"path\":\".\",\"pattern\":\"\\\"profileUrl\\\"|\\\"articleUrl\\\"\"}"}}]
-->

The type error shows the deeper problem: the index card is rendering a booking CTA from **summary data**, but `HotelSummary` does not carry `links`. The fix belongs at the data contract layer, not in the component alone.

---

{"content":"import React from 'react';\nimport { \n  LineChart, \n  Line, \n  XAxis, \n  YAxis, \n  CartesianGrid, \n  Tooltip, \n  ResponsiveContainer,\n  ReferenceDot\n} from 'recharts';\nimport type { PricingIntelligence } from '../../data/types';\nimport styles from './PriceCurveChart.module.css';\n\ninterface PriceCurveChartProps {\n  pricing: PricingIntelligence;\n}\n\nexport const PriceCurveChart: React.FC<PriceCurveChartProps> = ({ pricing }) => {\n  if (pricing.status !== 'complete' || !pricing.dataPoints || pricing.dataPoints.length === 0) {\n    return (\n      <div className={styles.emptyState}>\n        <p>Pricing intelligence collection is {pricing.status === 'collecting' ? 'in progress' : 'not started'} for this property.</p>\n        {pricing.dmwInterpretation && <p className={styles.interpretation}>{pricing.dmwInterpretation}</p>}\n      </div>\n    );\n  }\n\n  // Format date for display\n  const formatDate = (dateStr: string) => {\n    const date = new Date(dateStr);\n    return date.toLocaleDateString('en-US', { month: 'short', day: 'numeric' });\n  };\n\n  const CustomTooltip = ({ active, payload }: any) => {\n    if (active && payload && payload.length) {\n      const point = payload[0].payload;\n      return (\n        <div className={styles.tooltip}>\n          <p className={styles.tooltipDate}>{formatDate(point.date)}</p>\n          <p className={styles.tooltipRate}>\n            {pricing.currency} {point.rate}\n          </p>\n          {point.notes && <p className={styles.tooltipNotes}>{point.notes}</p>}\n        </div>\n      );\n    }\n    return null;\n  };\n\n  return (\n    <div className={styles.container}>\n      <div className={styles.chartHeader}>\n        <div className={styles.chartMeta}>\n          <span className={styles.metaLabel}>Basis</span>\n          <span className={styles.metaValue}>{pricing.roomBasis || 'Standard Room'}</span>\n        </div>\n        <div className={styles.chartMeta}>\n          <span className={styles.metaLabel}>Median Rate</span>\n          <span className={styles.metaValue}>{pricing.currency} {pricing.medianObservedRate}</span>\n        </div>\n        <div className={styles.chartMeta}>\n          <span className={styles.metaLabel}>Highest Rate</span>\n          <span className={styles.metaValue}>{pricing.currency} {pricing.highestObservedRate}</span>\n        </div>\n      </div>\n      \n      <div className={styles.chartWrapper}>\n        <ResponsiveContainer width=\"100%\" height={300}>\n          <LineChart data={pricing.dataPoints} margin={{ top: 20, right: 30, left: 20, bottom: 20 }}>\n            <CartesianGrid strokeDasharray=\"3 3\" vertical={false} stroke=\"rgba(18, 18, 18, 0.1)\" />\n            <XAxis \n              dataKey=\"date\" \n              tickFormatter={formatDate} \n              axisLine={false} \n              tickLine={false} \n              tick={{ fontFamily: 'Inter', fontSize: 12, fill: '#777C70' }}\n              dy={10}\n            />\n            <YAxis \n              axisLine={false} \n              tickLine={false} \n              tick={{ fontFamily: 'Inter', fontSize: 12, fill: '#777C70' }}\n              tickFormatter={(val) => `${val}`}\n              dx={-10}\n            />\n            <Tooltip content={<CustomTooltip />} />\n            <Line \n              type=\"monotone\" \n              dataKey=\"rate\" \n              stroke=\"#B39A62\" \n              strokeWidth={2}\n              dot={{ r: 4, fill: '#B39A62', strokeWidth: 0 }}\n              activeDot={{ r: 6, fill: '#121212' }}\n            />\n            {/* Map event markers if they exist */}\n            {pricing.eventMarkers && pricing.eventMarkers.map((marker, idx) => {\n              const point = pricing.dataPoints.find(p => p.date === marker.date);\n              if (point) {\n                return (\n                  <ReferenceDot \n                    key={idx} \n                    x={marker.date} \n                    y={point.rate} \n                    r={6} \n                    fill=\"#6D292F\" \n                    stroke=\"none\"\n                  />\n                );\n              }\n              return null;\n            })}\n          </LineChart>\n        </ResponsiveContainer>\n      </div>\n\n      {pricing.dmwInterpretation && (\n        <div className={styles.interpretation}>\n          <h4>DMW Interpretation</h4>\n          <p>{pricing.dmwInterpretation}</p>\n        </div>\n      )}\n    </div>\n  );\n};\n","path":"/Users/stevenmorales/Development/hotel100/src/components/hotel/PriceCurveChart.tsx","size_bytes":4341,"truncated":false}

## Assistant

<!--prism:turn-meta
{"cost_usd":5.344927500000001,"elapsed_ms":189443,"mode":null,"model":"openai/gpt-5.4","total_tokens":2120336}
-->

I reviewed the last 10 commits, traced the changed caller paths, fixed the loose ends, and verified the project cleanly.

## Fixes Applied

- **`src/pages/CollectionIndex.tsx`**
  - Fixed the filtered results memo so the new **strategic lens** and **amenity** filters actually re-run the filter logic.
  - Problem: the UI state changed, but `useMemo` only depended on `allHotels`, `search`, `regionFilter`, and `priceFilter`, so the new filters could appear selected while results stayed stale.

- **`src/components/index/RankedHotelItem.tsx`**
  - Removed the dead **“Check Dates”** link.
  - Problem: the recent commit added a visible CTA with `href="#"`, but the summary model has no booking URL source. That created a misleading interaction and, when wired naively, a type error because `HotelSummary` does not include `links`.

- **`05-hotel-schema.json`**
  - Expanded the **`strategicLens` enum** to match the values now present in **`07-content/collections.json`**.
  - Problem: the schema update only added a partial enum set, while the data now contains additional values like:
    - `Luxury-brand real-estate platform`
    - `Palace as luxury ecosystem`
    - `View-led / location-scarce asset`
    - `Ultra-private mansion`
    - `Heritage conversion / reinvention`
    - `Design-led urban lifestyle hotel`
  - Without this, schema validation would reject legitimate current content.

- **`.oxlintrc.json`**
  - Added an Oxlint config to ignore generated/vendor directories:
    - `node_modules/**`
    - `.vite/**`
    - `dist/**`
  - Problem: lint was failing on third-party and generated files rather than project code, which made the verification gate noisy and unreliable.

- **`src/data/api.ts`**
  - Cleaned the unused destructure binding flagged by lint by renaming `hotels` to `_hotels`.

- **`src/pages/CollectionIndex.tsx`**
  - Cleaned the empty catch parameter flagged by lint.

## Why This Works

### The Problem
The recent commit set introduced three classes of loose ends:

- **Behavioral wiring gap**: new filter controls were added, but not fully connected to the memoized filtering pipeline.
- **UI/data contract mismatch**: a booking-style CTA was introduced without actual booking data in the summary layer.
- **Schema drift**: taxonomy values evolved in content faster than the JSON schema enum.

### The Solution
The fixes align each layer with its actual contract:

- The collection page now recomputes whenever the new filter state changes.
- The ranked card no longer advertises an action the data model cannot support.
- The schema now reflects the active taxonomy in the dataset.
- Lint now measures application code instead of vendor/cache output.

## Verification

- **Typecheck:** clean
- **Build:** clean
- **Lint:** clean

Build still emits a **bundle size warning** from Vite for a large production chunk, but it is a warning, not a correctness failure.

**Confidence: High**

**TL;DR:** The main regressions were stale collection filters, a dead “Check Dates” CTA, and a schema enum that no longer matched the dataset. I fixed all three and got typecheck, build, and lint clean.

\"DMW Visited\"\n           },\n-          \"inclusionRationale\": \"A high-consensus asset representing the pinnacle of hospitality in London.\",\n-          \"indicativeRate\": {\n-            \"currency\": \"USD\",\n-            \"amount\": 1258,\n-            \"label\": \"Observed Typical Rate\",\n-            \"basis\": \"per night\"\n+          \"analysis\": {\n+            \"hospitalityProposition\": \"A heritage Mayfair grand hotel that sells discretion, intimacy, and highly polished personal service over scale; the core promise is refined private-club luxury in a landmark London address.\",\n+            \"atmosphere\": \"Arrival feels hushed and controlled, with classic London restraint rather than spectacle; the crowd is typically composed of well-dressed international leisure guests, repeat elite travelers, and quietly powerful business figures.\",\n+            \"intendedClientele\": \"HNW leisure travelers, royalty-adjacent and sovereign wealth circles, C-suite executives, fashion and arts leaders, and repeat luxury travelers who value discretion more than conspicuous size.\",\n+            \"designLogic\": \"Interiors emphasize warm traditional luxury, polished materials, and a layered historic fabric; the design logic is to preserve heritage character while delivering contemporary five-star comfort in a compact urban footprint.\",\n+            \"locationLogic\": \"The address in Carlos Place/Mount Street, Mayfair places the hotel inside one of London’s most scarce luxury micro-markets, with immediate access to high-end retail, private clubs, galleries, and diplomatic/business nodes.\",\n+            \"revenueStrategy\": \"Revenue is driven by high ADR suites and rooms, signature dining, the destination bar, private events/boardrooms, and premium ancillary spend through spa, concierge, and in-room service.\",\n+            \"pricingPowerThesis\": \"The Connaught can command premium rates because it combines a rare Mayfair address, intense brand equity, high-touch service, and a reputation for discretion that outperforms many newer luxury competitors.\",\n+            \"competitiveMoat\": \"Its moat comes from historical legitimacy, a globally recognized bar and dining reputation, deep repeat-client loyalty, and a Mayfair location that is difficult to replicate or expand.\",\n+            \"investorQuestion\": \"How to preserve the hotel’s elite aura and pricing power while protecting the fabric, service standards, and relevance of a very old, relatively small, ultra-premium city asset.\"\n+          },\n+          \"insiderReport\": {\n+            \"unGoogleableHistory\": \"The property began life in the early nineteenth century as the Prince of Saxe-Coburg Hotel before being renamed The Connaught in 1917 during the First World War, a name change tied to royal sentiment and British identity.\",\n+            \"operationalQuirks\": \"The hotel is known for highly discreet service, careful arrival handling, and an old-school luxury cadence that prioritizes privacy and seamlessness over theatrical front-of-house showmanship.\",\n+            \"famousGuests\": \"The hotel has long attracted royalty, senior political figures, wealthy international families, fashion insiders, and arts-world regulars; its reputation is built as much on discretion as on public-name clientele.\",\n+            \"theTrueBestRoom\": \"The best-booked inventory is generally the top-end suite stock facing Mayfair/Carlos Place or higher-floor quiet interior vantage points; the safest advice is to request the most recently refreshed suite tier and avoid low-floor street-facing rooms if silence matters.\",\n+            \"powerDynamics\": \"The hotel operates within the Maybourne ecosystem and is owned/managed through that group structure, giving it the benefits of a portfolio operator while aligning its strategic identity with sibling luxury properties in London.\"\n           },\n           \"pricingIntelligence\": {\n             \"status\": \"complete\",\n@@ -259,230 +333,339 @@\n             \"eventMarkers\": [],\n             \"limitations\": \"5-point institutional forward curve.\"\n           },\n-          \"specialPackages\": [\n-            {\n-              \"id\": \"pkg-1\",\n-              \"title\": \"Valentine's Day Retreat\",\n-              \"description\": \"Experience the ultimate romance in Mayfair. Your suite will be transformed with a path of velvet rose petals leading to the bedroom, where a chilled bottle of Laurent-Perrier Grand Siècle and bespoke chocolate truffles await. Includes breakfast in bed and a late 2 PM checkout.\",\n-              \"price\": 2450,\n-              \"currency\": \"GBP\",\n-              \"validity\": \"Feb 10 - Feb 16\",\n-              \"imageUrl\": \"https://images.unsplash.com/photo-1574621100236-d25b61edbebc?q=80&w=1200&auto=format&fit=crop\",\n-              \"linkUrl\": \"https://www.the-connaught.co.uk/\"\n-            }\n-          ],\n-          \"insiderReport\": {\n-            \"unGoogleableHistory\": \"The Connaught began in 1815 as the Prince of Saxe-Coburg Hotel, later taking the Connaught name in 1917; its modern reputation was shaped by general manager Rudolph Richard, who reportedly ran it like a high-end private house with unusually exacting service standards. It also sits inside a long, unusually bitter ownership saga: the hotel moved from the Savoy Group era into the Maybourne orbit, then became entangled in years of shareholder warfare and later Qatari-backed control of the parent platform. The lore here is less about a single scandal than a century of reinvention, elite discretion, and ownership drama behind polished Mayfair calm.\",\n-            \"operationalQuirks\": \"A notable quirk is the emphasis on discretion and private access: reports describe a private entrance that lets guests avoid public sightlines on arrival and departure. The hotel has long been treated more like an urban private residence than a conventional large luxury hotel, which shows up in the service culture and the way it handles high-profile arrivals. The property is managed within the Maybourne system, so the operational style is tightly controlled and highly standardized across the group, but with The Connaught retaining a more old-world, low-visibility cadence than flashier rivals.\",\n-            \"famousGuests\": \"The Connaught has drawn royals, financiers, and other privacy-sensitive elites rather than celebrity spectacle; its brand is built around discreet, high-net-worth clientele. Publicly cited material is stronger on the hotel’s elite social gravity than on a verified A-list guest ledger, which is itself part of the point: the most interesting clientele are typically the ones not being announced. The hotel’s historical positioning and Mayfair address make it a known hideout for global power brokers, aristocrats, and wealthy families.\",\n-            \"theTrueBestRoom\": \"Book the largest suite category with a private entrance or the top-tier corner suites rather than a standard room; the real advantage at The Connaught is privacy, separation, and arrival discretion, not just square footage. If the goal is maximum anonymity, ask specifically for a suite that supports private entry and avoids the more public-facing circulation routes. The hotel’s value proposition is strongest in its upper suites and residence-like accommodations, where the old-house feel and seclusion matter more than a generic “best view.”\",\n-            \"powerDynamics\": \"The operating brand is Maybourne Hotel Group, which owns and manages The Connaught as part of a tightly controlled luxury portfolio. The asset has been through a long chain of control disputes and refinancing events: it moved from the Savoy Group to Maybourne, then into a structure influenced by Irish investors, the Barclay family’s interests, and ultimately Qatari-backed control through Constellation Hotels/Qatar Holding. The interesting friction is that the hotel’s day-to-day management identity is Maybourne, but the economic power has repeatedly shifted above it through shareholder battles and refinancing pressure, meaning the real story is often at the ownership layer rather than the lobby.\"\n+          \"inclusionRationale\": \"A high-consensus asset representing the pinnacle of hospitality in London.\",\n+ {\n-            \"currency\": \"USD\",\n-            \"amount\": 1285,\n-            \"label\": \"Observed Typical Rate\",\n-            \"basis\": \"per night\"\n+          \"analysis\": {\n+            \"hospitalityProposition\": \"A historic London grande dame that sells legacy, theatrical public spaces, and a concentrated luxury ecosystem rather than merely rooms; the promise is iconic address, polished service, and destination F&B under a globally recognised luxury brand.\",\n+            \"atmosphere\": \"Arrival is formal, hushed, and high-gloss, with a strong sense of heritage staging; the mood shifts between the restrained elegance of the public spaces and the more energetic bar-and-restaurant scenes, with acoustics and crowd profile reflecting a mix of leisure, dining, and occasion-driven traffic.\",\n+            \"intendedClientele\": \"HNW leisure travellers, transatlantic luxury regulars, fashion and media figures, diplomatic guests, legacy brand loyalists, and executives who value centrality plus prestige.\",\n+            \"designLogic\": \"Edwardian bones and Art-Deco styling are preserved and selectively modernised; the design logic is to contrast rich materials, symmetry, and landmark public rooms with refreshed guestroom comfort and contemporary utility.\",\n+            \"locationLogic\": \"The Savoy occupies one of London’s most scarce and recognisable river-adjacent central positions, bridging the West End and the Strand/Covent Garden corridor with immediate access to theatre, finance, dining, and transport.\",\n+            \"revenueStrategy\": \"Monetisation is driven by premium room rates, signature dining, high-yield bars, event/banqueting, and ancillary spend from spa, breakfast, parking, and suite-level service upgrades.\",\n+            \"pricingPowerThesis\": \"The hotel can charge a premium because it combines a globally legible name, river-facing centrality, historic narrative, and a dense luxury experience ecosystem that competitors cannot fully replicate.\",\n+            \"competitiveMoat\": \"The moat is the combination of heritage status, myth-making, central address scarcity, bar-and-restaurant gravity, and the cachet of staying in one of London’s most storied hotels.\",\n+            \"investorQuestion\": \"How to preserve the Savoy’s iconic status and rate premium while continuously refreshing rooms, protecting brand theatre, and sustaining demand across business and leisure cycles.\"\n+          },\n+          \"insiderReport\": {\n+            \"unGoogleableHistory\": \"The Savoy’s modern legend is inseparable from its role as a social stage for aristocrats, performers, and power brokers; it has repeatedly been repositioned through major restorations rather than replaced, which is part of why its aura remains unusually durable.\",\n+            \"operationalQuirks\": \"Suite guests can receive highly personalised butler support, and the hotel’s public areas are structured to manage a mix of residents, restaurant guests, and bar traffic without collapsing the sense of exclusivity.\",\n+            \"famousGuests\": \"The hotel is strongly associated with figures such as Frank Sinatra, Noel Coward, Richard Harris, and numerous politicians, royals, musicians, and film stars; its bars and suites have long functioned as a cultural salon.\",\n+            \"theTrueBestRoom\": \"The most desirable bookings are generally the river-facing suites and higher-category rooms in the newer or fully refreshed inventory, especially the Personality Suites and Royal Suite tier; rooms to avoid are lower-floor, less private categories facing the busiest city aspects or any unrefreshed inventory during phased refurbishment periods.\",\n+            \"powerDynamics\": \"The asset has been owned by international luxury-hotel capital and operated by Fairmont under the Accor umbrella; that creates a classic owner-operator split where {"content":"import type { IndexData, HotelProfile, HotelSummary, Collection } from './types';\nimport indexDataRaw from '../../07-content/hotels.json';\nimport collectionsDataRaw from '../../07-content/collections.json';\n\nconst indexData = indexDataRaw as unknown as IndexData;\nconst collectionsData = collectionsDataRaw as unknown as { collections: Collection[] };\n\n/**\n * Returns the index data containing the ranking of 100 hotels (prototype).\n */\nexport function getIndexData(): Omit<IndexData, 'hotels'> {\n  const { hotels: _hotels, ...meta } = indexData;\n  return meta;\n}\n\n/**\n * Returns all hotels from the index.\n */\nexport function getAllHotels(): HotelSummary[] {\n  return indexData.hotels;\n}\n\n/**\n * Returns a collection by its slug.\n */\nexport function getCollection(slug: string): Collection | undefined {\n  return collectionsData.collections.find(c => c.slug === slug);\n}\n\n/**\n * Returns a specific hotel profile by its slug.\n */\nexport function getHotelProfile(slug: string): HotelProfile | undefined {\n  // Try to find the hotel in the master index\n  const hotel = indexData.hotels.find(h => h.slug === slug);\n  \n  if (hotel) {\n    return hotel as unknown as HotelProfile;\n  }\n  return undefined;\n}\n","path":"/Users/stevenmorales/Development/hotel100/src/data/api.ts","size_bytes":1201,"truncated":false}

{"content":"export type Archetype = \n  | 'Urban Grand Hotel'\n  | 'Urban Lifestyle Hotel'\n  | 'Urban Resort'\n  | 'Resort'\n  | 'Heritage Hotel'\n  | 'Boutique Hotel'\n  | 'Wellness Retreat'\n  | 'Wilderness Lodge'\n  | 'Private-Island Hotel'\n  | 'Members-Club Hybrid'\n  | 'Branded Residence Ecosystem';\n\nexport type Distinction = \n  | 'Best for Business Travel'\n  | 'Pricing Power'\n  | 'Hospitality Ecosystem'\n  | 'Independent Excellence'\n  | 'Urban Resort'\n  | 'Best Repositioning'\n  | 'Best New Hotel'\n  | 'Best Hotel Restaurant'\n  | 'Most Investable Hospitality Concept'\n  | 'DMW One to Watch';\n\nexport interface Location {\n  city: string;\n  country: string;\n  countryCode?: string | null;\n  region: 'Europe' | 'North America' | 'Latin America and Caribbean' | 'Middle East' | 'Africa' | 'Asia' | 'Oceania';\n  neighbourhood?: string | null;\n  displayLocation: string;\n  addressLine1?: string | null;\n  addressLine2?: string | null;\n  stateOrProvince?: string | null;\n  postalCode?: string | null;\n}\n\nexport interface Amenity {\n  id: string;\n  label: string;\n  icon?: string | null; // Lucide icon name\n  category?: 'Food and Drink' | 'Wellness' | 'Business' | 'Transport' | 'Rooms' | 'Family' | 'Accessibility' | 'Leisure' | 'Other';\n  available?: boolean;\n  detail?: string | null;\n  venueName?: string | null;\n}\n\nexport interface Image {\n  id: string;\n  url: string;\n  alt: string;\n  caption?: string | null;\n  credit?: string | null;\n  isHero?: boolean;\n}\n\nexport interface DimensionScore {\n  label: string;\n  score: number;\n  maxScore: number;\n  weight: number; // percentage (e.g., 15)\n}\n\nexport interface Scorecard {\n  totalScore: number;\n  dimensions: DimensionScore[];\n  confidence: 'DMW Researched' | 'DMW Visited' | 'DMW Revisited';\n}\n\nexport interface HotelSummary {\n  id: string;\n  slug: string;\n  edition: number;\n  rank: number;\n  rankStatus?: 'illustrative' | 'final';\n  name: string;\n  location: Location;\n  archetype: Archetype;\n  publicationStatus: 'draft' | 'review' | 'published' | 'archived' | 'prototype-ready' | 'research-draft';\n  featured: boolean;\n  strategicLens?: string;\n  dmwJudgement?: string | null;\n  assessmentPendingLabel?: string | null;\n  indicativeRate?: {\n    currency: string;\n    amount: number;\n    label: string;\n    basis: string;\n  } | null;\n  businessTravelSuitability?: string;\n  scores?: Scorecard | null;\n  essentialAmenities: Amenity[];\n  distinctions: Distinction[];\n  hasFieldReport?: boolean;\n  hasStrategicFeature: boolean;\n  hasPricingAnalysis: boolean;\n  primaryImage?: Image | null;\n  profileUrl: string;\n  articleUrl?: string | null;\n}\n\nexport interface FieldReport {\n  id: string;\n  visitDate: string; // YYYY-MM\n  nights: number;\n  approximateRatePaid?: number | null;\n  currency?: string | null;\n  roomCategory?: string | null;\n  travelPurpose?: string | null;\n  arrivalObservation: string;\n  roomObservation: string;\n  serviceObservation: string;\n  atmosphereObservation: string;\n  amenitiesUsed: string[];\n  whatWorked: string;\n  whatDisappointed: string;\n  returnIntention: string;\n  thesisConfirmation: string;\n}\n\nexport interface PricePoint {\n  date: string; // YYYY-MM-DD\n  rate: number;\n  available?: boolean | null;\n  roomCategory?: string | null;\n  rateType?: string | null;\n  notes?: string | null;\n  tenor?: string | null;\n}\n\nexport interface EventMarker {\n  date: string;\n  label: string;\n  eventType?: string | null;\n}\n\nexport interface PricingIntelligence {\n  status: 'not-started' | 'collecting' | 'complete';\n  currency?: string | null;\n  roomBasis?: string | null;\n  occupancyBasis?: string | null;\n  taxesIncluded?: boolean | null;\n  observationStart?: string | null;\n  observationEnd?: string | null;\n  collectionDate?: string | null;\n  sourceMethod?: string | null;\n  medianObservedRate?: number | null;\n  lowestObservedRate?: number | null;\n  highestObservedRate?: number | null;\n  bestValuePeriod?: string | null;\n  peakPeriod?: string | null;\n  weekdayWeekendObservation?: string | null;\n  dmwInterpretation?: string | null;\n  dataPoints: PricePoint[];\n  eventMarkers: EventMarker[];\n  limitations?: string | null;\n}\n\nexport interface SpecialPackage {\n  id: string;\n  title: string;\n  description: string;\n  price?: number | null;\n  currency?: string | null;\n  validity?: string | null;\n  imageUrl?: string | null;\n  linkUrl?: string | null;\n}\n\nexport interface string | null;\n    serviceSpeed?: string | null;\n    breakfastPracticality?: string | null;\n    soloTravellerSuitability?: string | null;\n  };\n  \n  rooms: {\n    summary?: string | null;\n    minimumSizeSqm?: number | null;\n    maximumStandardRoomSizeSqm?: number | null;\n    categories: string[];\n    workspace?: string | null;\n    storage?: string | null;\n    bathroom?: string | null;\n    lighting?: string | null;\n    technology?: string | null;\n    views?: string | null;\n    spatialStrength?: string | null;\n    spatialCompromise?: string | null;\n  };\n\n  pricingIntelligence?: PricingIntelligence | null;\n  insiderReport?: InsiderReport | null;\n  \n  competitiveSet: Array<{\n    hotelId?: string | null;\n    name: string;\n    location?: string | null;\n    relativePricePosition?: string | null;\n    locationDifference?: string | null;\n    amenityDifference?: string | null;\n    brandDifference?: string | null;\n    dmwInterpretation?: string | null;\n  }>;\n\n  fieldReports: FieldReport[];\n  images: Image[];\n  specialPackages?: SpecialPackage[] | null;\n  \n  links: {\n    officialWebsite?: string | null;\n    bookingUrl?: string | null;\n    pressPage?: string | null;\n  };\n  \n  sources: Array<{\n    type: 'official' | 'third-party' | 'financial' | 'pricing' | 'other';\n    name: string;\n    url?: string | null;\n    dateAccessed?: string | null;\n    notes?: string | null;\n  }>;\n}\n\nexport interface IndexData {\n  edition: number;\n  editionStatus: string;\n  title: string;\n  publisher: string;\n  prototypeNotice: string;\n  hotels: HotelSummary[];\n}\n\nexport interface Collection {\n  slug: string;\n  title: string;\n  edition: string;\n  description: string;\n  hotels: HotelSummary[];\n}\n","path":"/Users/stevenmorales/Development/hotel100/src/data/types.ts","size_bytes":8468,"truncated":false}

{"content":"import React, { useState } from 'react';\nimport { Link } from 'react-router-dom';\nimport { X, Check, Send } from 'lucide-react';\nimport { getAllHotels } from '../../data/api';\nimport type { HotelSummary } from '../../data/types';\nimport styles from './AskDmwDrawer.module.css';\n\ninterface AskDmwDrawerProps {\n  isOpen: boolean;\n  onClose: () => void;\n  initialQuery?: string;\n}\n\nconst PRESET_QUERIES = [\n  'Zurich business hotel under $500 with gym',\n  'Mayfair suites with private butler service',\n  'Swiss Lakes & Léman luxury resort sanctuary',\n  'Best high-ADR hotel in Dubai with beach',\n  'Paris palace hotel with Michelin dining',\n];\n\nexport const AskDmwDrawer: React.FC<AskDmwDrawerProps> = ({ isOpen, onClose, initialQuery = '' }) => {\n  const [query, setQuery] = useState(initialQuery);\n  const [userEmail, setUserEmail] = useState('');\n  const [submitted, setSubmitted] = useState(false);\n\n  if (!isOpen) return null;\n\n  const allHotels = getAllHotels();\n\n  // Simple dynamic decision query matcher across city, name, amenities, archetype, and rates\n  const filteredHotels = query.trim()\n    ? allHotels.filter((hotel: HotelSummary) => {\n        const q = query.toLowerCase();\n        const matchesName = hotel.name.toLowerCase().includes(q);\n        const matchesCity = hotel.location.city.toLowerCase().includes(q);\n        const matchesCountry = hotel.location.country.toLowerCase().includes(q);\n        const matchesArchetype = (hotel.archetype || '').toLowerCase().includes(q);\n        const matchesLens = (hotel.strategicLens || '').toLowerCase().includes(q);\n\n        // Check amenities\n        const matchesAmenity = (hotel.essentialAmenities || []).some((a: { label: string }) => a.label.toLowerCase().includes(q));\n\n        // Check price terms (e.g. under 500)\n        let matchesPrice = true;\n        if (q.includes('under 500') || q.includes('under $500') || q.includes('under €500')) {\n          matchesPrice = !!(hotel.indicativeRate && hotel.indicativeRate.amount <= 500);\n        }\n\n        return (matchesName || matchesCity || matchesCountry || matchesArchetype || matchesLens || matchesAmenity) && matchesPrice;\n      }).slice(0, 5)\n    : allHotels.slice(0, 4);\n\n  const handleSubmit = (e: React.FormEvent) => {\n    e.preventDefault();\n    if (!userEmail) return;\n\n    // Simulate sending email inquiry to s.moralesmed@gmail.com\n    console.log(`Sending Ask DMW inquiry to s.moralesmed@gmail.com from ${userEmail} with query: \"${query}\"`);\n    setSubmitted(true);\n  };\n\n  return (\n    <>\n      <div className={styles.backdrop} onClick={onClose} aria-hidden=\"true\" />\n      <aside className={styles.drawer} aria-label=\"Ask DMW Advisory Drawer\">\n        <header className={styles.header}>\n          <div className={styles.titleGroup}>\n            <span className={styles.badge}>DMW Advisory Engine</span>\n            <h2 className={styles.title}>Ask DMW</h2>\n          </div>\n          <button type=\"button\" className={styles.closeButton} onClick={onClose} aria-label=\"Close drawer\">\n            <X size={18} />\n          </button>\n        </header>\n\n        <div className={styles.content}>\n          <span className={styles.sectionLabel}>Bespoke Travel Intent Prompts</span>\n          <div className={styles.presets}>\n            {PRESET_QUERIES.map((preset) => (\n              <button\n                key={preset}\n                type=\"button\"\n                className={styles.presetChip}\n                onClick={() => { setQuery(preset); setSubmitted(false); }}\n              >\n                {preset}\n              </button>\n            ))}\n          </div>\n\n          <div className={styles.searchBox}>\n            <input\n              type=\"text\"\n              className={styles.searchInput}\n              placeholder=\"e.g. Zurich business hotel under $500 with gym...\"\n              value={query}\n              onChange={(e) => { setQuery(e.target.value); setSubmitted(false); }}\n            />\n          </div>\n\n          <span className={styles.sectionLabel}>\n            {query.trim() ? `Recommended Shortlist (${filteredHotels.length} matched)` : 'Top Indexed Flagships'}\n          </span>\n\n          <div className={styles.resultsList}>\n            {filteredHotels.length > 0 ? (\n              filteredHotels.map((hotel: HotelSummary) => (\n                <Link\n                  key={hotel.id}\n                  to={hotel.profileUrl}\n                  className={styles.resultCard}\n                  onClick={onClose}\n                >\n                  <div className={styles.resultHeader}>\n                    <span className={styles.resultName}>{hotel.name}</span>\n                    <span className={styles.resultRank}>No. {hotel.rank}</span>\n                  </div>\n                  <span className={styles.resultLocation}>\n                    {hotel.location.displayLocation} • {hotel.indicativeRate ? `$${hotel.indicativeRate.amount}/night` : 'Inquire for rate'}\n                  </span>\n                  <p className={styles.resultRationale}>\n                    {hotel.dmwJudgement || `${hotel.archetype} asset delivering high proposition coherence.`}\n                  </p>\n                </Link>\n              ))\n            ) : (\n              <p style={{ fontSize: '0.9rem', color: 'var(--color-stone)' }}>\n                No direct matches found for \"{query}\". Try searching by city (e.g. Zurich, London, Paris, Geneva) or amenity.\n              </p>\n            )}\n          </div>\n\n          <div className={styles.emailSection}>\n            <span className={styles.sectionLabel}>Save Shortlist or Inquire with DMW Desk</span>\n\n            {submitted ? (\n              <div className={styles.successMessage}>\n                <Check size={18} style={{ color: 'var(--color-antique-gold)', marginBottom: 4 }} />\n                <p><strong>Shortlist Inquiry Sent!</strong></p>\n                <p>Your request has been routed to DMW Advisory (s.moralesmed@gmail.com). A senior travel strategist will follow up shortly.</p>\n              </div>\n            ) : (\n              <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className={styles.emailForm}>\n                <input\n                  type=\"email\"\n                  required\n                  className={styles.emailInput}\n                  placeholder=\"Enter your email (e.g. principal@familyoffice.com)\"\n                  value={userEmail}\n                  onChange={(e) => setUserEmail(e.target.value)}\n                />\n                <button type=\"submit\" className={styles.submitButton}>\n                  Send Query to DMW Advisory <Send size={14} style={{ marginLeft: 6 }} />\n                </button>\n              </form>\n            )}\n          </div>\n        </div>\n      </aside>\n    </>\n  );\n};\n","path":"/Users/stevenmorales/Development/hotel100/src/components/shared/AskDmwDrawer.tsx","size_bytes":6723,"truncated":false}

{"content":"import React, { useEffect, useMemo, useState } from 'react';\nimport { Navigate, useParams } from 'react-router-dom';\nimport { Container } from '../components/shared';\nimport { RankedHotelItem } from '../components/index/RankedHotelItem';\nimport { AskDmwDrawer } from '../components/shared/AskDmwDrawer';\nimport { getCollection } from '../data/api';\nimport styles from './CollectionIndex.module.css';\n\nconst PRICE_BANDS = [\n  { label: 'All prices', min: 0, max: Infinity },\n  { label: 'Under $500', min: 0, max: 500 },\n  { label: 'Under $1,000', min: 0, max: 1000 },\n  { label: 'Under $1,500', min: 0, max: 1500 },\n  { label: '$1,500+', min: 1500, max: Infinity },\n];\n\nconst AMENITIES = [\n  { id: 'gym', label: 'Gym' },\n  { id: 'parking', label: 'Parking' },\n  { id: 'restaurant', label: 'Restaurant' },\n];\n\nexport const CollectionIndex: React.FC = () => {\n  const { slug = '' } = useParams<{ slug: string }>();\n  const collection = getCollection(slug);\n  const allHotels = collection?.hotels ?? [];\n\n  const [askDmwOpen, setAskDmwOpen] = useState(false);\n\n  const [search, setSearch] = useState('');\n  const [regionFilter, setRegionFilter] = useState('All regions');\n  const [priceFilter, setPriceFilter] = useState('All prices');\n  const [lensFilter, setLensFilter] = useState('All lenses');\n  const [amenityFilters, setAmenityFilters] = useState<string[]>([]);\n  const [filtersOpen, setFiltersOpen] = useState(false);\n  const [savedPresetActive, setSavedPresetActive] = useState(false);\n\n  useEffect(() => {\n    try {\n      const saved = localStorage.getItem('dmw_saved_filters');\n      const parsed = saved ? JSON.parse(saved) : null;\n\n      if (parsed?.slug === slug) {\n        setSearch(parsed.search || '');\n        setRegionFilter(parsed.regionFilter || 'All regions');\n        setPriceFilter(parsed.priceFilter || 'All prices');\n        setLensFilter(parsed.lensFilter || 'All lenses');\n        setAmenityFilters(parsed.amenityFilters || []);\n        setSavedPresetActive(true);\n        return;\n      }\n    } catch {\n      // A malformed local preset should never block the collection.\n    }\n\n    setSearch('');\n    setRegionFilter('All regions');\n    setPriceFilter('All prices');\n    setLensFilter('All lenses');\n    setAmenityFilters([]);\n    setSavedPresetActive(false);\n    setFiltersOpen(false);\n  }, [slug]);\n\n  const regions = useMemo(\n    () => ['All regions', ...Array.from(new Set(allHotels.map((hotel) => hotel.location.region).filter(Boolean)))],\n    [allHotels],\n  );\n\n  const lenses = useMemo(\n    () => ['All lenses', ...Array.from(new Set(allHotels.map((hotel) => hotel.strategicLens).filter(Boolean)))],\n    [allHotels],\n  );\n\n  const filteredHotels = useMemo(() => {\n    const query = search.trim().toLowerCase();\n    const selectedBand = PRICE_BANDS.find((band) => band.label === priceFilter);\n\n    return allHotels.filter((hotel) => {\n      const matchesSearch =\n        !query ||\n        hotel.name.toLowerCase().includes(query) ||\n        hotel.location.city.toLowerCase().includes(query) ||\n        hotel.location.displayLocation.toLowerCase().includes(query);\n\n      const matchesRegion =\n        regionFilter === 'All regions' || hotel.location.region === regionFilter;\n\n      const rate = hotel.indicativeRate?.amount;\n      const matchesPrice =\n        !selectedBand ||\n        selectedBand.label === 'All prices' ||\n        (typeof rate === 'number' && rate >= selectedBand.min && rate <= selectedBand.max);\n\n      const matchesLens =\n        lensFilter === 'All lenses' || hotel.strategicLens === lensFilter;\n\n      const matchesAmenities = amenityFilters.every((amenityId) =>\n        hotel.essentialAmenities?.some(\n          (amenity) => amenity.id === amenityId && amenity.available === true,\n        ),\n      );\n\n      return matchesSearch && matchesRegion && matchesPrice && matchesLens && matchesAmenities;\n    });\n  }, [allHotels, search, regionFilter, priceFilter, lensFilter, amenityFilters]);\n\n  if (!collection) {\n    return <Navigate to=\"/collections/the-global-100\" replace />;\n  }\n\n  const activeFilterCount =\n    Number(regionFilter !== 'All regions') +\n    Number(priceFilter !== 'All prices') +\n    Number(lensFilter !== 'All lenses') +\n    amenityFilters.length;\n\n  const toggleAmenity = (id: string) => {\n    setAmenityFilters((current) =>\n      current.includes(id) ? current.filter((item) => item !== id) : [...current, id],\n    );\n  };\n\n  const clearFilters = () => {\n    setSearch('');\n    setRegionFilter('All regions');\n    setPriceFilter('All prices');\n    setLensFilter('All lenses');\n    setAmenityFilters([]);\n  };\n\n  const savePreset = () => {\n    localStorage.setItem(\n      'dmw_saved_filters',\n      JSON.stringify({ slug, search, regionFilter, priceFilter, lensFilter, amenityFilters }),\n    );\n    setSavedPresetActive(true);\n  };\n\n  const clearPreset = () => {\n    localStorage.removeItem('dmw_saved_filters');\n    clearFilters();\n    setSavedPresetActive(false);\n  };\n\n  return (\n    <main className={styles.page}>\n      <header className={styles.masthead}>\n        <Container variant=\"wide\">\n          <span className={styles.editionLabel}>DMW Hotels 100 · 2026 Edition</span>\n          <div className={styles.mastheadGrid}>\n            <h1 className={styles.title}>{collection.title}</h1>\n            <div className={styles.mastheadAside}>\n              <span className={styles.asideLabel}>About this edition</span>\n              <p className={styles.subtitle}>{collection.description}</p>\n              <div className={styles.collectionMeta}>\n                <span>{allHotels.length} hotels</span>\n                <span>Independently assessed</span>\n              </div>\n            </div>\n          </div>\n        </Container>\n      </header>\n\n      <section className={styles.discovery} aria-label=\"Search and filter hotels\">\n        <Container variant=\"wide\">\n          <div className={styles.discoveryBar}>\n            <label className={styles.searchField}>\n              <span className=\"visually-hidden\">Search hotels or cities</span>\n              <span className={styles.searchMark} aria-hidden=\"true\">⌕</span>\n              <input\n                type=\"search\"\n                placeholder=\"Search hotels or cities\"\n                value={search}\n                onChange={(event) => setSearch(event.target.value)}\n              />\n            </label>\n\n            <div className={styles.discoveryActions}>\n              <button\n                type=\"button\"\n                className={`${styles.filterToggle} ${filtersOpen ? styles.filterToggleActive : ''}`}\n                onClick={() => setFiltersOpen((open) => !open)}\n                aria-expanded={filtersOpen}\n              >\n                Filters {activeFilterCount > 0 && <span>{activeFilterCount}</span>}\n              </button>\n              <button\n                type=\"button\"\n                className={styles.askButton}\n                onClick={() => setAskDmwOpen(true)}\n              >\n                <span>Ask DMW</span>\n                <small>AI Advisory</small>\n              </button>\n            </div>\n          </div>\n\n          {filtersOpen && (\n            <div className={styles.filterPanel}>\n              <label className={styles.selectField}>\n                <span>Region</span>\n                <select value={regionFilter} onChange={(event) => setRegionFilter(event.target.value)}>\n                  {regions.map((region) => <option key={region} value={region}>{region}</option>)}\n                </select>\n              </label>\n\n              <label className={styles.selectField}>\n                <span>Price</span>\n                <select value={priceFilter} onChange={(event) => setPriceFilter(event.target.value)}>\n                  {PRICE_BANDS.map((band) => <option key={band.label} value={band.label}>{band.label}</option>)}\n                </select>\n              </label>\n\n              <label className={styles.selectField}>\n                <span>Strategic lens</span>\n                <select value={lensFilter} onChange={(event) => setLensFilter(event.target.value)}>\n                  {lenses.map((lens) => <option key={lens} value={lens}>{lens}</option>)}\n                </select>\n              </label>\n\n              <fieldset className={styles.amenityField}>\n                <legend>Amenities</legend>\n                <div>\n                  {AMENITIES.map((amenity) => (\n                    <button\n                      type=\"button\"\n                      key={amenity.id}\n                      className={amenityFilters.includes(amenity.id) ? styles.amenityActive : ''}\n                      onClick={() => toggleAmenity(amenity.id)}\n                      aria-pressed={amenityFilters.includes(amenity.id)}\n                    >\n                      {amenity.label}\n                    </button>\n                  ))}\n                </div>\n              </fieldset>\n\n              <div className={styles.filterUtilities}>\n                <button type=\"button\" onClick={clearFilters}>Reset filters</button>\n                <button type=\"button\" onClick={savedPresetActive ? clearPreset : savePreset}>\n                  {savedPresetActive ? 'Clear saved view' : 'Save this view'}\n                </button>\n              </div>\n            </div>\n          )}\n        </Container>\n      </section>\n\n      <section className={styles.results}>\n        <Container variant=\"wide\">\n          <div className={styles.resultsHeader}>\n            <span>\n              Showing <strong>{filteredHotels.length}</strong> of {allHotels.length} hotels\n            </span>\n            {activeFilterCount > 0 && (\n              <button type=\"button\" onClick={clearFilters}>Clear filters</button>\n            )}\n          </div>\n\n          <div className={styles.list}>\n            {filteredHotels.length > 0 ? (\n              filteredHotels.map((hotel) => <RankedHotelItem key={hotel.id} hotel={hotel} />)\n            ) : (\n              <div className={styles.empty}>\n                <h2>No matching hotels</h2>\n                <p>Try broadening the location, price or strategic lens.</p>\n                <button type=\"button\" onClick={clearFilters}>Reset all filters</button>\n              </div>\n            )}\n          </div>\n        </Container>\n      </section>\n      <AskDmwDrawer isOpen={askDmwOpen} onClose={() => setAskDmwOpen(false)} />\n    </main>\n  );\n};\n","path":"/Users/stevenmorales/Development/hotel100/src/pages/CollectionIndex.tsx","size_bytes":10349,"truncated":false}
