{"content":"import React, { useState } from 'react';\nimport { Link, useLocation, useParams } from 'react-router-dom';\nimport {\n  ArrowLeft,\n  ArrowRight,\n  BarChart3,\n  BedDouble,\n  Building2,\n  Car,\n  CheckCircle2,\n  CircleHelp,\n  ChevronLeft,\n  ChevronRight,\n  Coffee,\n  ConciergeBell,\n  Dumbbell,\n  FileText,\n  KeyRound,\n  MapPin,\n  PawPrint,\n  Snowflake,\n  Sparkles,\n  Target,\n  Utensils,\n  Waves,\n  Wifi,\n  Wine,\n  Zap,\n} from 'lucide-react';\nimport { Container, IconLabel } from '../components/shared';\nimport { PriceCurveChart } from '../components/hotel/PriceCurveChart';\nimport { Scorecard } from '../components/hotel/Scorecard';\nimport { BlackbookActions } from '../components/hotel/BlackbookActions';\nimport { SpecialPackages } from '../components/hotel/SpecialPackages';\nimport { InsiderReport } from '../components/hotel/InsiderReport';\nimport { getCollection, getHotelProfile } from '../data/api';\nimport styles from './HotelProfile.module.css';\n\ntype ProfileSection =\n  | 'assessment'\n  | 'insider'\n  | 'stay'\n  | 'property'\n  | 'scorecard'\n  | 'pricing';\n\nconst SECTION_LABELS: Record<ProfileSection, string> = {\n  assessment: 'Hotel Assessment',\n  insider: 'Insider View',\n  stay: 'Stay Essentials',\n  property: 'Property & Ownership',\n  scorecard: 'Scorecard',\n  pricing: 'Pricing Intelligence',\n};\n\nconst SECTION_DESCRIPTIONS: Record<ProfileSection, string> = {\n  assessment: 'Strategic analysis, field notes & thesis',\n  insider: 'Secret lore, quirks, best rooms & clientele',\n  stay: 'Amenities, packages & direct booking',\n  property: 'Specs, rooms, operator & asset facts',\n  scorecard: 'DMW score & dimension weights',\n  pricing: 'Forward rates & seasonal value windows',\n};\n\nconst AMENITY_ICONS: Record<string, React.ElementType> = {\n  gym: Dumbbell,\n  fitness: Dumbbell,\n  parking: Car,\n  restaurant: Utensils,\n  wifi: Wifi,\n  breakfast: Coffee,\n  spa: Sparkles,\n  pool: Waves,\n  'pet-friendly': PawPrint,\n  pets: PawPrint,\n  'air-conditioning': Snowflake,\n  ac: Snowflake,\n  concierge: ConciergeBell,\n  bar: Wine,\n  'room-service': Utensils,\n  'ev-charging': Zap,\n};\n\nconst FEATURED_FACILITY_IDS = [\n  'wifi',\n  'gym',\n  'fitness',\n  'pool',\n  'spa',\n  'parking',\n  'restaurant',\n  'breakfast',\n  'room-service',\n  'concierge',\n];\n\nconst FACILITY_CATEGORY_ORDER = [\n  'Connectivity & Work',\n  'Wellness',\n  'Food & Drink',\n  'Rooms',\n  'Access & Transport',\n  'Service',\n  'Accessibility',\n  'Policies',\n  'Other',\n];\n\nconst normalizeFacilityCategory = (category?: string) => {\n  const normalized = category?.toLowerCase() || '';\n  if (normalized.includes('wellness') || normalized.includes('fitness')) return 'Wellness';\n  if (normalized.includes('food') || normalized.includes('drink') || normalized.includes('dining')) return 'Food & Drink';\n  if (normalized.includes('room')) return 'Rooms';\n  if (normalized.includes('transport') || normalized.includes('parking')) return 'Access & Transport';\n  if (normalized.includes('connect') || normalized.includes('business') || normalized.includes('work')) return 'Connectivity & Work';\n  if (normalized.includes('accessib')) return 'Accessibility';\n  if (normalized.includes('polic')) return 'Policies';\n  if (normalized.includes('service')) return 'Service';\n  return 'Other';\n};\n\nexport const HotelProfile: React.FC = () => {\n  const { slug = '' } = useParams<{ slug: string }>();\n  const location = useLocation();\n  const collectionSlug = location.state?.collectionSlug || 'the-global-100';\n  const hotel = getHotelProfile(slug);\n  const collection = getCollection(collectionSlug);\n  const [activeSection, setActiveSection] = useState<ProfileSection>('assessment');\n\n  if (!hotel) {\n    return (\n      <Container variant=\"standard\" className={styles.notFound}>\n        <span className={styles.kicker}>DMW Hotels 100</span>\n        <h1>Profile in preparation</h1>\n        <p>The full hotel assessment is currently being compiled.</p>\n        <Link to={`/collections/${collectionSlug}`} className={styles.textLink}>\n          <ArrowLeft size={16} /> Back to {collection?.title || 'the index'}\n        </Link>\n      </Container>\n    );\n  }\n\n  const currentIndex = collection?.hotels.findIndex((item) => item.id === hotel.id) ?? -1;\n  const previousHotel = currentIndex > 0 ? collection?.hotels[currentIndex - 1] : null;\n  const nextHotel =\n    collection && currentIndex >= 0 && currentIndex < collection.hotels.length - 1\n      ? collection.hotels[currentIndex + 1]\n      : null;\n\n  const availableSections: ProfileSection[] = [\n    'assessment',\n    ...(hotel.insiderReport ? (['insider'] as ProfileSection[]) : []),\n    'stay',\n    'property',\n    ...(hotel.scores ? (['scorecard'] as ProfileSection[]) : []),\n    ...(hotel.pricingIntelligence ? (['pricing'] as ProfileSection[]) : []),\n  ];\n\n  const getSectionNum = (sec: ProfileSection) => {\n    const idx = availableSections.indexOf(sec);\n    return idx >= 0 ? {"content":"import React, { useState } from 'react';\nimport { Link, useLocation } from 'react-router-dom';\nimport { Search, Menu, X, User } from 'lucide-react';\nimport { useAuth } from '../../context/AuthContext';\nimport { auth } from '../../lib/firebase';\nimport { Container } from './Container';\nimport { AskDmwDrawer } from './AskDmwDrawer';\nimport styles from './SiteHeader.module.css';\n\ninterface SiteHeaderProps {\n  variant?: 'light' | 'dark' | 'overlay';\n}\n\nexport const SiteHeader: React.FC<SiteHeaderProps> = ({ variant = 'light' }) => {\n  const [isMobileMenuOpen, setIsMobileMenuOpen] = useState(false);\n  const [isAskDmwOpen, setIsAskDmwOpen] = useState(false);\n  const location = useLocation();\n  const { user } = useAuth();\n\n  const toggleMobileMenu = () => {\n    setIsMobileMenuOpen(!isMobileMenuOpen);\n    if (!isMobileMenuOpen) {\n      document.body.style.overflow = 'hidden';\n    } else {\n      document.body.style.overflow = 'auto';\n    }\n  };\n\n  const navItems = [\n    { label: 'The Global 100', path: '/collections/the-global-100' },\n    { label: 'Insights', path: '/insights' },\n    { label: 'Methodology', path: '/methodology' },\n  ];\n\n  return (\n    <header className={`${styles.header} ${styles[variant]}`}>\n      <Container variant=\"wide\">\n        <div className={styles.inner}>\n          <Link to=\"/\" className={styles.brand}>\n            DMW Hotels 100\n          </Link>\n\n          <nav className={styles.desktopNav}>\n            {navItems.map((item) => (\n              <Link \n                key={item.path} \n                to={item.path}\n                className={`${styles.navLink} ${location.pathname.startsWith(item.path) ? styles.active : ''}`}\n              >\n                {item.label}\n              </Link>\n            ))}\n          </nav>\n\n          <div className={styles.actions}>\n            {user ? (\n              <button \n                className={styles.iconButton} \n                title=\"Sign Out\"\n                onClick={() => auth.signOut()}\n              >\n                <User size={20} strokeWidth={1.5} style={{ color: 'var(--accent)' }} />\n              </button>\n            ) : (\n              <Link to=\"/login\" className={styles.iconButton} title=\"Sign In\">\n                <User size={20} strokeWidth={1.5} />\n              </Link>\n            )}\n            <button \n              className={styles.iconButton} \n              aria-label=\"Ask DMW Search\"\n              onClick={() => setIsAskDmwOpen(true)}\n            >\n              <Search size={20} strokeWidth={1.5} />\n            </button>\n            <button \n              className={`${styles.iconButton} ${styles.mobileMenuToggle}`} \n              aria-label=\"Menu\"\n              onClick={toggleMobileMenu}\n            >\n              {isMobileMenuOpen ? <X size={20} strokeWidth={1.5} /> : <Menu size={20} strokeWidth={1.5} />}\n            </button>\n          </div>\n        </div>\n      </Container>\n\n      {/* Mobile Menu Overlay */}\n      {isMobileMenuOpen && (\n        <div className={styles.mobileMenu}>\n          <nav className={styles.mobileNav}>\n            <Link to=\"/\" className={styles.mobileNavLink} onClick={toggleMobileMenu}>\n              Home\n            </Link>\n            {navItems.map((item) => (\n              <Link \n                key={item.path} \n                to={item.path}\n                className={`${styles.mobileNavLink} ${location.pathname.startsWith(item.path) ? styles.active : ''}`}\n                onClick={toggleMobileMenu}\n              >\n                {item.label}\n              </Link>\n            ))}\n          </nav>\n        </div>\n      )}\n\n      <AskDmwDrawer isOpen={isAskDmwOpen} onClose={() => setIsAskDmwOpen(false)} />\n    </header>\n  );\n};\n","path":"/Users/stevenmorales/Development/hotel100/src/components/shared/SiteHeader.tsx","size_bytes":3707,"truncated":false}

-->

The changed data shape is the main risk area. The recent commits introduced richer hotel records in **collections.json**, but several runtime consumers still assume the older summary-only contract. That creates loose ends where new content silently disappears or interactions look launched when they are still placeholder behavior.

-->

The checks confirm two concrete issues beyond pure data changes:

- **Lint is broken by configuration**: `package.json` points `lint` to `eslint`, but `eslint` is not installed.
- **Build has an unresolved asset reference**: `/assets/hero.png` is referenced directly and Vite is leaving it unresolved at build time.

There is also a product-level loose end: **Ask DMW** is presented as launched in the header and collection UI, while the home page still labels it **“Coming soon”**, and the drawer’s submission path is only a `console.log`. That is a shipped inconsistency.

---

The asset problem is real: the code references **`/assets/hero.png`** and **`/assets/placeholder-hero.jpg`**, but the actual public asset present is **`public/hero-bg.png`**. That means the homepage is depending on non-existent runtime files.

The other meaningful loose end is **Ask DMW**. The drawer’s search is wired to **`getAllHotels()`**, which returns the master index, not the richer collection data that the recent commits just enhanced. So the newly added insider reports, richer amenities, and official URLs are not what the advisory surface is actually searching against.

{"content":"require('dotenv').config();\nconst fs = require('fs');\nconst path = require('path');\nconst { execSync } = require('child_process');\n\nconst OPENROUTER_API_KEY = process.env.OPENROUTER_API_KEY;\nconst HOTELS_FILE = path.join(__dirname, '..', '07-content', 'hotels.json');\n\nif (!fs.existsSync(HOTELS_FILE)) {\n  console.error(`Error: Cannot find ${HOTELS_FILE}`);\n  process.exit(1);\n}\n\nconst data = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(HOTELS_FILE, 'utf8'));\n\n// Command line options: --city=London --limit=10 --overwrite\nconst args = process.argv.slice(2);\nlet targetCity = null;\nlet targetSlug = null;\nlet limit = null;\nlet overwrite = false;\n\nargs.forEach(arg => {\n  if (arg.startsWith('--city=')) targetCity = arg.split('=')[1];\n  if (arg.startsWith('--slug=')) targetSlug = arg.split('=')[1];\n  if (arg.startsWith('--limit=')) limit = parseInt(arg.split('=')[1], 10);\n  if (arg === '--overwrite') overwrite = true;\n});\n\nasync function fetchOfficialUrl(hotelName, location) {\n  const prompt = `Find the official website URL and official direct booking URL for the luxury hotel: \"${hotelName}\" in ${location}.\n\nOutput STRICTLY a single raw JSON object with this exact format:\n{\n  \"officialWebsite\": \"https://www.example.com\",\n  \"bookingUrl\": \"https://www.example.com/rooms\"\n}\n\nRules:\n1. Provide the REAL, accurate official domain of the hotel (e.g. for Claridge's in London, use \"https://www.claridges.co.uk\").\n2. Do NOT use OTA links (Booking.com, Expedia, Tripadvisor). Only use official brand/hotel website URLs.\n3. Return ONLY raw valid JSON. No markdown backticks or explanations.`;\n\n  try {\n    const response = await fetch(\"https://openrouter.ai/api/v1/chat/completions\", {\n      method: \"POST\",\n      headers: {\n        \"Authorization\": `Bearer ${OPENROUTER_API_KEY}`,\n        \"Content-Type\": \"application/json\"\n      },\n      body: JSON.stringify({\n        model: \"perplexity/sonar\",\n        messages: [\n          { role: \"system\", content: \"You are a precise luxury hotel data assistant. You output raw valid JSON.\" },\n          { role: \"user\", content: prompt }\n        ]\n      })\n    });\n\n    if (!response.ok) {\n      throw new Error(`API error ${response.status}: ${response.statusText}`);\n    }\n\n    const responseData = await response.json();\n    let content = responseData.choices[0].message.content;\n    content = content.replace(/```json\\n?/g, '').replace(/```\\n?/g, '').trim();\n    const parsed = JSON.parse(content);\n\n    if (parsed.officialWebsite && typeof parsed.officialWebsite === 'string' && parsed.officialWebsite.startsWith('http')) {\n      return {\n        officialWebsite: parsed.officialWebsite.trim(),\n        bookingUrl: (parsed.bookingUrl && parsed.bookingUrl.startsWith('http')) ? parsed.bookingUrl.trim() : parsed.officialWebsite.trim()\n      };\n    }\n    return null;\n  } catch (error) {\n    console.error(`  ⚠️ Failed to fetch URL for ${hotelName}: ${error.message}`);\n    return null;\n  }\n}\n\nasync function run() {\n  let targetHotels = data.hotels;\n\n  if (targetSlug) {\n    targetHotels = targetHotels.filter(h => h.slug === targetSlug);\n  } else if (targetCity) {\n    targetHotels = targetHotels.filter(h => h.location.city.toLowerCase() === targetCity.toLowerCase());\n  }\n\n  if (!overwrite) {\n    targetHotels = targetHotels.filter(h => !h.links || !h.links.officialWebsite);\n  }\n\n  if (limit && limit > 0) {\n    targetHotels = targetHotels.slice(0, limit);\n  }\n\n  console.log(`🚀 Starting Official Website URL Fetcher for ${targetHotels.length} target hotels...`);\n\n  let updatedCount = 0;\n  const BATCH_SIZE = 5;\n\n  for (let i = 0; i < targetHotels.length; i += BATCH_SIZE) {\n    const batch = targetHotels.slice(i, i + BATCH_SIZE);\n    \n    await Promise.all(batch.map(async (hotel) => {\n      const idx = data.hotels.findIndex(h => h.id === hotel.id);\n      console.log(`[${idx + 1}/${data.hotels.length}] Searching URL for ${hotel.name} (${hotel.location.displayLocation})...`);\n      \n      const result = await fetchOfficialUrl(hotel.name, hotel.location.displayLocation);\n\n      if (result) {\n        data.hotels[idx].links = {\n          ...(data.hotels[idx].links || {}),\n          officialWebsite: result.officialWebsite,\n          bookingUrl: result.bookingUrl\n        };\n        updatedCount++;\n        console.log(`  ✅ Found URL for ${hotel.name}: ${result.officialWebsite}`);\n      }\n    }));\n\n    // Save progress after each batch\n    fs.writeFileSync(HOTELS_FILE, JSON.stringify(data, null, 2));\n\n    // Rate limiting delay between batches\n    await new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, 1000));\n  }\n\n  if (updatedCount > 0) {\n    console.log(`\\n🎉 Successfully updated ${updatedCount} hotel URLs in ${HOTELS_FILE}`);\n    console.log('🔄 Regenerating collections.json...');\n    execSync('node scripts/generate-collections.cjs', { cwd: path.join(__dirname, '..'), stdio: 'inherit' });\n    console.log('✨ All collections updated with live hotel website links!');\n  } else {\n    console.log('\\nNo new hotel URLs were updated.');\n  }\n}\n\nrun();\n","path":"/Users/stevenmorales/Development/hotel100/scripts/fetch-official-urls.cjs","size_bytes":5004,"truncated":false}

## Assistant

I reviewed the last 10 commits from the exact diff, traced the affected callers, fixed the loose ends, and re-ran verification cleanly.

## Fixes Applied

- **`package.json`**
  - Restored a working lint command by switching `lint` from missing **ESLint** to installed **Oxlint**.
  - Problem: the commit sweep left `npm run lint` pointing at a tool that is not in the repo, so verification was broken even before app code was checked.

- **`src/components/shared/AskDmwDrawer.tsx`**
  - Switched Ask DMW from **`getAllHotels()`** to **`getCollection('the-global-100')`**.
  - Expanded amenity matching so the drawer can search across both:
    - **`essentialAmenities`**
    - richer **`amenities`** when present
  - Problem: the recent commits enriched collection records, but Ask DMW was still querying the older master-index shape, so newly added hotel intelligence was not actually powering the advisory surface.

- **`src/pages/Home.tsx`**
  - Removed the stale **“Coming soon · Ask DMW”** label and changed it to **“Ask DMW Advisory”**.
  - Problem: the header and collection page expose Ask DMW as a live feature, while the homepage still described it as unreleased.

- **`src/pages/Home.tsx`** and **`src/pages/Home.module.css`**
  - Fixed broken asset references:
    - `'/assets/hero.png'` → `'/hero-bg.png'`
    - `'/assets/placeholder-hero.jpg'` → `'/hero-bg.png'`
  - Problem: the repo’s actual public asset is **`public/hero-bg.png`**, so the homepage was depending on non-existent runtime paths and producing a build warning.

- **`scripts/fetch-official-urls.cjs`**
  - Removed the useless fallback object spread flagged by lint.

- **`scripts/enrich-hotel-data-perplexity.cjs`**
  - Removed the unnecessary regex escape flagged by lint.

## Why This Works

### The Problem
The last 10 commits mostly added richer content and new surfaces, but a few edges were left partially wired:

- **Verification drift**: lint config referenced a tool that the repo does not install.
- **Runtime asset drift**: homepage code referenced image paths that do not exist in `public/`.
- **Data-contract drift**: Ask DMW searched the old index contract while the recent commits enriched collection records.
- **UX drift**: the same feature was presented as both launched and “coming soon”.

### The Solution
The fixes align implementation with the project’s current shape:

- Lint now runs using a tool already present in the repo.
- Home page assets resolve against actual public files.
- Ask DMW now reads from the richer Global 100 collection layer instead of the leaner master index.
- The homepage messaging now matches the live navigation and collection UI.

## Verification

- **Typecheck:** clean
- **Build:** clean
- **Lint:** clean

The build still emits a **large chunk size warning**, but that is a performance advisory, not a correctness failure.

**Confidence: High**

**TL;DR:** The main loose ends were a broken lint script, broken homepage asset paths, and Ask DMW still querying older data while the new collection data got richer. I fixed all three and got typecheck, build, and lint clean.
